1→/**
     2→ * ModeSelector Component
     3→ *
     4→ * A responsive mode selector that automatically switches to icon-only mode
     5→ * when there isn't enough space to display text labels without truncation.
     6→ */
     7→
     8→import React, { useRef, useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
     9→import { cn } from '@/shared/components/ui/contracts/cn';
    10→import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipTrigger } from '@/shared/components/ui/tooltip';
    11→
    12→interface ModeSelectorItem {
    13→  id: string;
    14→  label: string;
    15→  icon: React.ReactNode;
    16→  onClick: () => void;
    17→}
    18→
    19→interface ModeSelectorProps {
    20→  items: ModeSelectorItem[];
    21→  activeId: string;
    22→  className?: string;
    23→}
    24→
    25→export const ModeSelector: React.FC<ModeSelectorProps> = ({
    26→  items,
    27→  activeId,
    28→  className,
    29→}) => {
    30→  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    31→  const measureRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    32→  const [showIconsOnly, setShowIconsOnly] = useState(false);
    33→
    34→  // Calculate minimum width needed for text mode
    35→  // Each button needs: icon (14px) + gap (6px) + text width + padding (24px horizontal)
    36→  // Plus gaps between buttons
    37→  const checkIfTextFits = useCallback(() => {
    38→    const container = containerRef.current;
    39→    const measureDiv = measureRef.current;
    40→    if (!container || !measureDiv) return;
    41→
    42→    // Get the available width (container width minus padding and gaps)
    43→    const containerWidth = container.clientWidth;
    44→    const padding = 8; // p-1 = 4px each side
    45→    const gaps = (items.length - 1) * 4; // gap-1 = 4px between items
    46→    const availableWidth = containerWidth - padding - gaps;
    47→
    48→    // Measure the hidden text spans to get required width
    49→    const spans = measureDiv.querySelectorAll('span');
    50→    let totalRequiredWidth = 0;
    51→
    52→    spans.forEach((span) => {
    53→      // Each button needs: padding (24px) + icon (14px) + gap (6px) + text width
    54→      const textWidth = span.scrollWidth;
    55→      const buttonWidth = 24 + 14 + 6 + textWidth;
    56→      totalRequiredWidth += buttonWidth;
    57→    });
    58→
    59→    // If we need more width than available, show icons only
    60→    const needsIconsOnly = totalRequiredWidth > availableWidth;
    61→    setShowIconsOnly(needsIconsOnly);
    62→  }, [items]);
    63→
    64→  useEffect(() => {
    65→    const container = containerRef.current;
    66→    if (!container) return;
    67→
    68→    // Initial check
    69→    checkIfTextFits();
    70→
    71→    // Set up ResizeObserver
    72→    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => {
    73→      checkIfTextFits();
    74→    });
    75→
    76→    resizeObserver.observe(container);
    77→
    78→    return () => {
    79→      resizeObserver.disconnect();
    80→    };
    81→  }, [checkIfTextFits]);
    82→
    83→  return (
    84→    <>
    85→      {/* Hidden measurement div - always renders text to measure */}
    86→      <div
    87→        ref={measureRef}
    88→        className="absolute opacity-0 pointer-events-none whitespace-nowrap text-sm"
    89→        aria-hidden="true"
    90→      >
    91→        {items.map((item) => (
    92→          <span key={item.id} className="inline-block px-1">
    93→            {item.label}
    94→          </span>
    95→        ))}
    96→      </div>
    97→
    98→      {/* Visible selector */}
    99→      <div
   100→        ref={containerRef}
   101→        className={cn(
   102→          "flex gap-1 border border-border rounded-lg overflow-hidden bg-muted/30 p-1",
   103→          className
   104→        )}
   105→      >
   106→        {items.map((item) => {
   107→          const button = (
   108→            <button
   109→              key={item.id}
   110→              onClick={item.onClick}
   111→              className={cn(
   112→                "flex-1 min-w-0 flex items-center justify-center transition-all rounded overflow-hidden",
   113→                showIconsOnly ? "p-2" : "gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm",
   114→                activeId === item.id
   115→                  ? "bg-background text-foreground font-medium shadow-sm"
   116→                  : "text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground hover:bg-muted/50"
   117→              )}
   118→            >
   119→              <span className={cn("flex-shrink-0", showIconsOnly ? "[&>svg]:h-4 [&>svg]:w-4" : "[&>svg]:h-3.5 [&>svg]:w-3.5")}>
   120→                {item.icon}
   121→              </span>
   122→              {!showIconsOnly && (
   123→                <span className="truncate">
   124→                  {item.label}
   125→                </span>
   126→              )}
   127→            </button>
   128→          );
   129→
   130→          // Wrap in tooltip when showing icons only
   131→          if (showIconsOnly) {
   132→            return (
   133→              <Tooltip key={item.id}>
   134→                <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   135→                  {button}
   136→                </TooltipTrigger>
   137→                <TooltipContent side="bottom" className="text-xs">
   138→                  {item.label}
   139→                </TooltipContent>
   140→              </Tooltip>
   141→            );
   142→          }
   143→
   144→          return button;
   145→        })}
   146→      </div>
   147→    </>
   148→  );
   149→};
   150→

   271→              )}
   272→              style={{ minHeight: '55dvh' }}
   273→            >
   274→              <InlineEditSidebar
   275→                variant="mobile"
   276→                model={sidebarModel}
   277→                onClose={onClose}
   278→                onNavigateToGeneration={onNavigateToGeneration}

   281→          </div>
   282→        </TooltipProvider>
   283→    );
   284→  }
   285→
   286→  return (

   288→      <div className="w-full h-full flex bg-transparent overflow-hidden">

   290→            className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center relative bg-black rounded-l-xl overflow-hidden"
   291→            style={{ width: '60%', height: '100%' }}

   297→            className={cn(

1→/**
     2→ * useImageEditOrchestrator
     3→ *
     4→ * Composes all image-edit mode hooks (inpainting, magic edit, reposition, img2img)
     5→ * and builds the ImageEditContext value. Extracted from ImageLightbox to keep it
     6→ * under 400 lines.
     7→ *
     8→ * Inputs: media, variant state, edit-settings persistence, lora management, upscale state.
     9→ * Outputs: imageEditValue (for context), mode flags, and generation handlers.
    10→ */
    11→
    12→import { useMemo, useCallback } from 'react';
    13→import type { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
    14→import type { ImageEditState } from '../contexts/ImageEditContext';
    15→import type { EditAdvancedSettings, EditMode, LoraMode, QwenEditModel } from './editSettingsTypes';
    16→import type { UseLoraManagerReturn } from '@/shared/hooks/useLoraManager';
    17→import type { LoraModel } from '@/shared/components/LoraSelectorModal';
    18→import type { EditMode as InpaintingEditMode } from './inpainting/types';
    19→
    20→import {
    21→  useInpainting,
    22→} from './useInpainting';
    23→import { useEditSettingsSync } from './useEditSettingsSync';
    24→import { useMagicEditMode } from './useMagicEditMode';
    25→import { useRepositionMode } from './useRepositionMode';
    26→import { useImg2ImgMode } from './useImg2ImgMode';
    27→
    28→// ============================================================================
    29→// Props
    30→// ============================================================================
    31→
    32→interface UseImageEditOrchestratorProps {
    33→  // Media & project context
    34→  mediaContext: {
    35→    media: GenerationRow;
    36→    selectedProjectId: string | null;
    37→    actualGenerationId: string | null;
    38→    shotId?: string;
    39→    toolTypeOverride?: string;
    40→    autoEnterInpaint: boolean;
    41→    thumbnailUrl?: string;
    42→  };
    43→
    44→  // Image display context
    45→  displayContext: {
    46→    imageDimensions: { width: number; height: number } | null;
    47→    imageContainerRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>;
    48→    effectiveImageUrl: string;
    49→  };
    50→
    51→  // Variant state context (from useSharedLightboxState)
    52→  variantContext: {
    53→  activeVariant: {
    54→    id?: string | null;
    55→    location?: string | null;
    56→    thumbnail_url?: string | null;
    57→    params?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
    58→  } | null;
    59→  setActiveVariantId: (id: string) => void;
    60→  refetchVariants: () => void;
    61→  };
    62→
    63→  // Edit settings persistence context (from useEditSettingsPersistence)
    64→  settingsContext: {
    65→    loraMode: LoraMode;
    66→    setLoraMode: (mode: LoraMode) => void;
    67→    customLoraUrl: string;
    68→    setCustomLoraUrl: (url: string) => void;
    69→    prompt: string;
    70→    setPrompt: (prompt: string) => void;
    71→    numGenerations: number;
    72→    setNumGenerations: (num: number) => void;
    73→    img2imgStrength: number;

    75→    img2imgEnablePromptExpansion: boolean;

    77→    img2imgPrompt: string;
    78→    setImg2imgPrompt: (prompt: string) => void;
    79→    img2imgPromptHasBeenSet: boolean;
    80→    editModeLoRAs: Array<{ url: string; strength: number }> | undefined;
    81→    createAsGeneration: boolean;
    82→    setCreateAsGeneration: (value: boolean) => void;
    83→    advancedSettings: EditAdvancedSettings;
    84→    setAdvancedSettings: (updates: Partial<EditAdvancedSettings>) => void;
    85→    qwenEditModel: QwenEditModel;
    86→    setQwenEditModel: (model: QwenEditModel) => void;
    87→    editMode: EditMode;
    88→    setEditMode: (mode: EditMode) => void;

    91→  };
    92→
    93→  // LoRA context
    94→  loraContext: {
    95→    effectiveEditModeLoRAs: Array<{ url: string; strength: number }> | undefined;
    96→    availableLoras?: LoraModel[];
    97→  };
    98→}
    99→
   100→// ============================================================================
   101→// Return Type
   102→// ============================================================================
   103→
   104→interface UseImageEditOrchestratorReturn {
   105→  // Context value (pass to ImageEditProvider)
   106→  imageEditValue: ImageEditState;

   108→  // Mode state (for layout, panel, and shell decisions)
   109→  isInpaintMode: boolean;

   111→  isSpecialEditMode: boolean;

   113→
   114→  // Mode entry/exit (for panel mode restore and shared state)

   117→
   118→  // Generation handlers (passed to EditModePanel)
   119→  handleUnifiedGenerate: () => Promise<void>;

   121→  handleGenerateReposition: () => Promise<void>;
   122→  handleSaveAsVariant: () => Promise<void>;

   124→
   125→  // Img2Img LoRA manager (passed to EditModePanel)

   128→}
   129→
   130→// ============================================================================
   131→// Hook
   132→// ============================================================================
   133→
   134→export function useImageEditOrchestrator({
   135→  mediaContext,
   136→  displayContext,
   137→  variantContext,
   138→  settingsContext,
   139→  loraContext,
   140→}: UseImageEditOrchestratorProps): UseImageEditOrchestratorReturn {
   141→  const {
   142→    media,
   143→    selectedProjectId,

   171→    img2imgEnablePromptExpansion: persistedImg2imgEnablePromptExpansion,
   172→    setImg2imgEnablePromptExpansion: setPersistedImg2imgEnablePromptExpansion,
   173→    img2imgPrompt: persistedImg2imgPrompt, setImg2imgPrompt: setPersistedImg2imgPrompt,

   181→  } = settingsContext;
   182→
   183→  const toInpaintingEditMode = useCallback((mode: EditMode): InpaintingEditMode => {
   184→    if (mode === 'inpaint' || mode === 'annotate' || mode === 'text') {
   185→      return mode;
   186→    }
   187→    return 'text';

   189→
   190→  // ========================================

   213→
   214→  const {

   228→    setIsEraseMode,

   238→    handleGenerateInpaint,

   249→
   250→  // ========================================
   251→  // EDIT SETTINGS SYNC

   253→
   254→  useEditSettingsSync({

   265→    setInpaintNumGenerations,

   267→    setPersistedEditMode: (mode) => setPersistedEditMode(mode),
   268→    setPersistedNumGenerations,
   269→    setPersistedPrompt,
   270→  });
   271→
   272→  const handleExitInpaintMode = useCallback(() => {
   273→    setIsInpaintMode(false);

   275→
   276→  // ========================================

   280→  const magicEditHook = useMagicEditMode({

   297→    toolTypeOverride,
   298→    isInSceneBoostEnabled: false,
   299→    setIsInSceneBoostEnabled: () => {},
   300→    activeVariantId: activeVariant?.id,
   301→    activeVariantLocation: activeVariant?.location,
   302→    createAsGeneration,
   303→    advancedSettings,
   304→    qwenEditModel,
   305→    enabled: true,
   306→  });
   307→
   308→  const {
   309→    isMagicEditMode,
   310→    setIsMagicEditMode,
   311→    isCreatingMagicEditTasks,
   312→    magicEditTasksCreated,
   313→    inpaintPanelPosition,
   314→    setInpaintPanelPosition,
   315→    handleEnterMagicEditMode,
   316→    handleExitMagicEditMode,
   317→    handleUnifiedGenerate,

   319→  } = magicEditHook;
   320→
   321→  // ========================================
   322→  // REPOSITION MODE HOOK
   323→  // ========================================
   324→
   325→  const repositionHook = useRepositionMode({
   326→    media,
   327→    selectedProjectId,

   332→    inpaintNumGenerations,
   333→    toolTypeOverride,
   334→    shotId,
   335→    onVariantCreated: setActiveVariantId,
   336→    refetchVariants,

   338→    advancedSettings,
   339→    activeVariantLocation: activeVariant?.location,
   340→    activeVariantId: activeVariant?.id,
   341→    activeVariantParams: activeVariant?.params as Record<string, unknown> | null,

   344→
   345→  const {

   350→    isSavingAsVariant,
   351→    saveAsVariantSuccess,

   362→  } = repositionHook;
   363→
   364→  // ========================================
   365→  // IMG2IMG MODE HOOK
   366→  // ========================================
   367→
   368→  const img2imgHook = useImg2ImgMode({
   369→    media,
   370→    selectedProjectId,
   371→    isVideo: false,
   372→    sourceUrlForTasks: effectiveImageUrl,
   373→    toolTypeOverride,
   374→    createAsGeneration,
   375→    availableLoras,

   379→    setEnablePromptExpansion: setPersistedImg2imgEnablePromptExpansion,
   380→    img2imgPrompt: persistedImg2imgPrompt,
   381→    setImg2imgPrompt: setPersistedImg2imgPrompt,
   382→    img2imgPromptHasBeenSet: persistedImg2imgPromptHasBeenSet,

   401→
   402→  // ========================================
   403→  // BUILD ImageEditContext VALUE

   405→
   406→  const isFlippedHorizontally = Boolean(repositionTransform?.flipH);

   408→
   409→  const imageEditValue = useMemo<ImageEditState>(() => ({

   426→
   427→    // Brush/Inpaint state
   428→    brushSize,
   429→    setBrushSize,

   431→    setIsEraseMode,
   432→    brushStrokes,
   433→
   434→    // Annotation state

   440→
   441→    // Undo/Clear
   442→    handleUndo,

   445→    // Canvas interaction
   446→    onStrokeComplete,

   449→    onTextModeHint,

   451→    getDeleteButtonPosition,

   453→    handleDeleteSelected,
   454→
   455→    // Reposition state + interaction handlers
   456→    repositionTransform,
   457→    hasTransformChanges,
   458→    isRepositionDragging,
   459→    repositionDragHandlers,
   460→    getTransformStyle,
   461→    setScale,
   462→    setRotation,
   463→    toggleFlipH,
   464→    toggleFlipV,
   465→    resetTransform,
   466→
   467→    // Display refs

   471→
   472→    // Panel UI state
   473→    inpaintPanelPosition,
   474→    setInpaintPanelPosition,
   475→
   476→    // Inpaint form
   477→    inpaintPrompt,
   478→    setInpaintPrompt,

   482→    // Img2Img form

1→/**
     2→ * usePanelModeRestore Hook
     3→ *
     4→ * Handles automatic restoration of edit/info panel mode when media changes.
     5→ * If the user was last in edit mode, automatically re-enters edit mode.
     6→ */
     7→
     8→import { useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
     9→
    10→interface UsePanelModeRestoreProps {
    11→  /** Current media ID for tracking changes */
    12→  mediaId: string;
    13→  /** Persisted panel mode from last used settings */
    14→  persistedPanelMode: 'edit' | 'info' | null | undefined;
    15→  /** Whether currently in special edit mode (image) */
    16→  isSpecialEditMode: boolean;
    17→  /** Whether currently in video edit mode */
    18→  isInVideoEditMode: boolean;
    19→  /** Whether initial video trim mode was requested */
    20→  initialVideoTrimMode?: boolean;
    21→  /** Whether auto-enter inpaint was requested */
    22→  autoEnterInpaint?: boolean;
    23→}
    24→
    25→interface UseVideoPanelModeRestoreProps extends UsePanelModeRestoreProps {
    26→  isVideo: true;
    27→  /** Handler to enter video edit mode */
    28→  handleEnterVideoEditMode: () => void;
    29→}
    30→
    31→interface UseImagePanelModeRestoreProps extends UsePanelModeRestoreProps {
    32→  isVideo: false;
    33→  /** Handler to enter magic edit mode (image) */
    34→  handleEnterMagicEditMode: () => void;
    35→}
    36→
    37→interface UsePanelModeRestoreReturn {
    38→  /** Whether panel mode has been restored for current media */
    39→  hasRestoredPanelMode: boolean;
    40→}
    41→
    42→/**
    43→ * Automatically restores the last used panel mode (edit/info) when opening a media item.
    44→ * Prevents loops by tracking restoration per-media and respecting explicit modes.
    45→ */
    46→export function usePanelModeRestore({
    47→  mediaId,
    48→  persistedPanelMode,
    49→  isVideo,
    50→  isSpecialEditMode,
    51→  isInVideoEditMode,
    52→  initialVideoTrimMode,
    53→  autoEnterInpaint,
    54→  ...modeHandlers
    55→}: UseVideoPanelModeRestoreProps | UseImagePanelModeRestoreProps): UsePanelModeRestoreReturn {
    56→  const hasRestoredPanelModeRef = useRef(false);
    57→  const enterEditMode = isVideo
    58→    ? modeHandlers.handleEnterVideoEditMode
    59→    : modeHandlers.handleEnterMagicEditMode;
    60→
    61→  // Main restoration effect
    62→  useEffect(() => {
    63→
    64→    // Only restore once per media (prevent loops)
    65→    if (hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current) {
    66→      return;
    67→    }
    68→
    69→    // Don't restore if initialVideoTrimMode or autoEnterInpaint is set (explicit modes take precedence)
    70→    if (initialVideoTrimMode || autoEnterInpaint) {
    71→      hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current = true;
    72→      return;
    73→    }
    74→
    75→    // Don't restore if already in edit mode
    76→    if (isSpecialEditMode || isInVideoEditMode) {
    77→      hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current = true;
    78→      return;
    79→    }
    80→
    81→    if (persistedPanelMode === 'edit') {
    82→      hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current = true;
    83→      setTimeout(() => enterEditMode(), 0);
    84→    } else {
    85→      hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current = true;
    86→    }
    87→  }, [
    88→    persistedPanelMode,
    89→    enterEditMode,
    90→    initialVideoTrimMode,
    91→    autoEnterInpaint,
    92→    isSpecialEditMode,
    93→    isInVideoEditMode,
    94→  ]);
    95→
    96→  // Reset restore flag when media changes
    97→  useEffect(() => {
    98→    hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current = false;
    99→  }, [mediaId]);
   100→
   101→  return {
   102→    hasRestoredPanelMode: hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current,
   103→  };
   104→}
   105→

1→/**
     2→ * useSharedLightboxState
     3→ *
     4→ * Consolidates hooks that are shared between ImageLightbox and VideoLightbox.
     5→ * This forms the foundation for splitting MediaLightbox into media-type-specific
     6→ * components while avoiding hook duplication.
     7→ *
     8→ * Included hooks (shared by both image and video):
     9→ * - Variants: useVariants, useVariantSelection, useVariantPromotion
    10→ * - Navigation: useLightboxNavigation, useSwipeNavigation
    11→ * - Star toggle: useStarToggle
    12→ * - References: useReferences, useJoinClips
    13→ * - Lineage: useGenerationLineage
    14→ * - Shot management: useShotPositioning, useShotCreation
    15→ * - Source generation: useSourceGeneration
    16→ * - Make main variant: useMakeMainVariant
    17→ * - Effective media: useEffectiveMedia
    18→ * - Layout mode: useLayoutMode
    19→ * - Button group props
    20→ *
    21→ * NOT included (media-type specific):
    22→ * - Image: useInpainting, useUpscale, useMagicEditMode, useRepositionMode, useImg2ImgMode
    23→ * - Video: useLightboxVideoMode, useVideoRegenerateMode
    24→ */

   290→/**
   291→ * Variants sub-facade: variant loading, selection, promotion.
   292→ * Usable standalone when callers only need variant state.
   293→ */
   294→export function useSharedVariantsState(input: UseSharedLightboxStateInput): SharedVariantsStateResult {
   295→  const {
   296→    media,
   297→    isFormOnlyMode,
   298→    variantFetchGenerationId,
   299→    initialVariantId,
   300→    selectedProjectId,
   301→  } = input.core;
   302→  const {
   303→    variants,
   304→    primaryVariant,
   305→    activeVariant,
   306→    isLoading: isLoadingVariants,
   307→    setActiveVariantId: rawSetActiveVariantId,
   308→    refetch: refetchVariants,
   309→    setPrimaryVariant,
   310→    deleteVariant,
   311→  } = useVariants({
   312→    generationId: variantFetchGenerationId,
   313→    enabled: !isFormOnlyMode,
   314→  });
   315→  const { setActiveVariantId: baseSetActiveVariantId, isViewingNonPrimaryVariant } = useVariantSelection({
   316→    media,
   317→    rawSetActiveVariantId,
   318→    activeVariant,
   319→    variants,
   320→    initialVariantId,
   321→  });
   322→  const intendedActiveVariantIdRef = useRef<string | null>(activeVariant?.id || null);
   323→  const {
   324→    promoteSuccess,
   325→    isPromoting,
   326→    handlePromoteToGeneration,
   327→    handleAddVariantAsNewGenerationToShot,
   328→  } = useVariantPromotion({
   329→    selectedProjectId,
   330→  });
   331→
   332→  useEffect(() => {
   333→    if (activeVariant?.id && activeVariant.id !== intendedActiveVariantIdRef.current) {
   334→      intendedActiveVariantIdRef.current = activeVariant.id;
   335→    }
   336→  }, [activeVariant?.id]);
   337→
   338→  const setActiveVariantId = useCallback((variantId: string) => {
   339→    intendedActiveVariantIdRef.current = variantId;
   340→    baseSetActiveVariantId(variantId);
   341→  }, [baseSetActiveVariantId]);
   342→
   343→  return {
   344→    section: {
   345→      list: variants || [],
   346→      primaryVariant,
   347→      activeVariant,
   348→      isLoading: isLoadingVariants,
   349→      setActiveVariantId,
   350→      refetch: refetchVariants,
   351→      setPrimaryVariant,
   352→      deleteVariant,
   353→      isViewingNonPrimaryVariant,
   354→      promoteSuccess,
   355→      isPromoting,
   356→      handlePromoteToGeneration,
   357→      handleAddVariantAsNewGenerationToShot,
   358→    },
   359→    intendedActiveVariantIdRef,
   360→    activeVariant,
   361→    primaryVariant,
   362→    isViewingNonPrimaryVariant,
   363→    setPrimaryVariant,
   364→    refetchVariants,
   365→  };
   366→}

   368→/**
   369→ * Navigation sub-facade: keyboard nav, swipe nav, safe close.
   370→ * Usable standalone when callers only need navigation controls.
   371→ */
   372→export function useLightboxNavigationModel(input: UseSharedLightboxStateInput): UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['navigation'] {
   373→  const { onClose, readOnly } = input.core;
   374→  const {
   375→    hasNext = false,
   376→    hasPrevious = false,
   377→    handleSlotNavNext,
   378→    handleSlotNavPrev,
   379→    swipeDisabled,
   380→    showNavigation,
   381→  } = input.navigation;
   382→  const { safeClose, activateClickShield } = useLightboxNavigation({
   383→    onNext: handleSlotNavNext,
   384→    onPrevious: handleSlotNavPrev,
   385→    onClose,
   386→  });
   387→  const swipeNavigation = useSwipeNavigation({
   388→    onSwipeLeft: () => {
   389→      if (hasNext) handleSlotNavNext();
   390→    },
   391→    onSwipeRight: () => {
   392→      if (hasPrevious) handleSlotNavPrev();
   393→    },
   394→    disabled: swipeDisabled || readOnly || !showNavigation,
   395→    hasNext,
   396→    hasPrevious,
   397→    threshold: 50,
   398→    velocityThreshold: 0.3,
   399→  });
   400→
   401→  return {
   402→    safeClose,
   403→    activateClickShield,
   404→    swipeNavigation,
   405→  };
   406→}
   407→export const useSharedNavigationState = useLightboxNavigationModel;
   408→
   409→/**
   410→ * Shot management sub-facade: positioning, creation, association state.
   411→ * Usable standalone when callers only need shot management.
   412→ */
   413→export function useLightboxShotActions(input: UseSharedLightboxStateInput): UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['shots'] {
   414→  const { media, selectedProjectId, onClose } = input.core;
   415→  const {
   416→    allShots,
   417→    onNavigateToShot,
   418→    onShotChange,
   419→    selectedShotId,
   420→    positionedInSelectedShot,
   421→    associatedWithoutPositionInSelectedShot,
   422→    optimisticPositionedIds,
   423→    optimisticUnpositionedIds,
   424→    onAddToShot,
   425→    onAddToShotWithoutPosition,
   426→    onShowTick,
   427→    onShowSecondaryTick,
   428→    onOptimisticPositioned,
   429→    onOptimisticUnpositioned,
   430→  } = input.shots;
   431→  const {
   432→    isCreatingShot,
   433→    quickCreateSuccess,
   434→    handleQuickCreateAndAdd,
   435→    handleQuickCreateSuccess,
   436→  } = useShotCreation({
   437→    media,
   438→    selectedProjectId,
   439→    allShots: allShots || [],
   440→    onNavigateToShot,
   441→    onClose,
   442→    onShotChange,
   443→  });
   444→  const computedPositionedInSelectedShot = useMemo(() => (
   445→    typeof positionedInSelectedShot === 'boolean' ? positionedInSelectedShot : undefined
   446→  ), [positionedInSelectedShot]);
   447→  const {
   448→    isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot,
   449→    isAlreadyAssociatedWithoutPosition,
   450→    handleAddToShot,
   451→    handleAddToShotWithoutPosition,
   452→  } = useShotPositioning({
   453→    media,
   454→    selectedShotId,
   455→    allShots: allShots || [],
   456→    positionedInSelectedShot: computedPositionedInSelectedShot,
   457→    associatedWithoutPositionInSelectedShot,
   458→    optimisticPositionedIds,
   459→    optimisticUnpositionedIds,
   460→    onNavigateToShot,
   461→    onClose,
   462→    onAddToShot,
   463→    onAddToShotWithoutPosition,
   464→    onShowTick,
   465→    onShowSecondaryTick,
   466→    onOptimisticPositioned,
   467→    onOptimisticUnpositioned,
   468→  });
   469→
   470→  return {
   471→    isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot,
   472→    isAlreadyAssociatedWithoutPosition,
   473→    handleAddToShot,
   474→    handleAddToShotWithoutPosition,
   475→    isCreatingShot,
   476→    quickCreateSuccess,
   477→    handleQuickCreateAndAdd,
   478→    handleQuickCreateSuccess,
   479→  };
   480→}
   481→export const useSharedShotState = useLightboxShotActions;

   483→function useSharedMakeMainVariantState(params: {
   484→  media: GenerationRow;
   485→  sourceGenerationData: GenerationRow | null;
   486→  isViewingNonPrimaryVariant: boolean;
   487→  activeVariant: GenerationVariant | null;
   488→  setPrimaryVariant: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
   489→  refetchVariants: () => void;
   490→  shotId?: string;
   491→  selectedShotId?: string;
   492→  onClose: () => void;
   493→}): UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['makeMainVariant'] {
   494→  const {
   495→    media,
   496→    sourceGenerationData,
   497→    isViewingNonPrimaryVariant,
   498→    activeVariant,
   499→    setPrimaryVariant,
   500→    refetchVariants,
   501→    shotId,
   502→    selectedShotId,
   503→    onClose,
   504→  } = params;
   505→  const canMakeMainVariantFromChild = !!sourceGenerationData && !!media.location;
   506→  const canMakeMainVariantFromVariant = isViewingNonPrimaryVariant && !!activeVariant?.location;
   507→  const canMakeMainVariant = canMakeMainVariantFromChild || canMakeMainVariantFromVariant;
   508→  const { isMakingMainVariant, handleMakeMainVariant } = useMakeMainVariant({
   509→    media,
   510→    sourceGenerationData,
   511→    canMakeMainVariantFromChild,
   512→    canMakeMainVariantFromVariant,
   513→    activeVariant,
   514→    setPrimaryVariant,
   515→    refetchVariants,
   516→    shotId,
   517→    selectedShotId,
   518→    onClose,
   519→  });
   520→
   521→  return {
   522→    canMake: canMakeMainVariant,
   523→    canMakeFromChild: canMakeMainVariantFromChild,
   524→    canMakeFromVariant: canMakeMainVariantFromVariant,
   525→    isMaking: isMakingMainVariant,
   526→    handle: handleMakeMainVariant,
   527→  };
   528→}

   618→export function useSharedLightboxState(input: UseSharedLightboxStateInput): UseSharedLightboxStateReturn {
   619→  const variantsState = useSharedVariantsState(input);
   620→  const interactionState = useSharedLightboxInteractionState(input, variantsState);
   621→  const presentationState = useLightboxPanelModel(input, variantsState, interactionState);
   622→
   623→  return {
   624→    variants: presentationState.variants,
   625→    intendedActiveVariantIdRef: presentationState.intendedActiveVariantIdRef,
   626→    navigation: presentationState.navigation,
   627→    star: interactionState.star,
   628→    references: interactionState.references,
   629→    lineage: interactionState.lineage,
   630→    shots: interactionState.shots,
   631→    sourceGeneration: interactionState.sourceGeneration,
   632→    makeMainVariant: interactionState.makeMainVariant,
   633→    effectiveMedia: presentationState.effectiveMedia,
   634→    layout: presentationState.layout,
   635→    buttonGroupProps: presentationState.buttonGroupProps,
   636→  };
   637→}

   642→  lineage: UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['lineage'];
   643→  shots: UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['shots'];
   644→  sourceGeneration: UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['sourceGeneration'];
   645→  makeMainVariant: UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['makeMainVariant'];
   646→}
   647→
   648→export function useSharedLightboxInteractionState(
   649→  input: UseSharedLightboxStateInput,
   650→  variantsState: SharedVariantsStateResult,
   651→): SharedLightboxInteractionState {
   652→  const star = useStarToggle({
   653→    media: input.core.media,
   654→    starred: input.starred,
   655→    shotId: input.shots.shotId,
   656→  });
   657→  const referencesState = useReferences({
   658→    media: input.core.media,
   659→    selectedProjectId: input.core.selectedProjectId,
   660→    isVideo: input.core.isVideo,
   661→    selectedShotId: input.shots.selectedShotId,
   662→  });
   663→  const joinState = useJoinClips({ media: input.core.media, isVideo: input.core.isVideo });
   664→  const lineageState = useGenerationLineage({
   665→    media: input.core.media,
   666→    enabled: !input.core.isFormOnlyMode,
   667→  });
   668→  const shots = useLightboxShotActions(input);
   669→  const { sourceGenerationData, sourcePrimaryVariant } = useSourceGeneration({
   670→    media: input.core.media,
   671→    onOpenExternalGeneration: input.onOpenExternalGeneration,
   672→  });
   673→  const makeMainVariant = useSharedMakeMainVariantState({
   674→    media: input.core.media,
   675→    sourceGenerationData,
   676→    isViewingNonPrimaryVariant: variantsState.isViewingNonPrimaryVariant,
   677→    activeVariant: variantsState.activeVariant,
   678→    setPrimaryVariant: variantsState.setPrimaryVariant,
   679→    refetchVariants: variantsState.refetchVariants,
   680→    shotId: input.shots.shotId,
   681→    selectedShotId: input.shots.selectedShotId,
   682→    onClose: input.core.onClose,
   683→  });
   684→
   685→  return {
   686→    star: {
   687→      localStarred: star.localStarred,
   688→      setLocalStarred: star.setLocalStarred,
   689→      toggleStarMutation: star.toggleStarMutation,
   690→      handleToggleStar: star.handleToggleStar,
   691→    },
   692→    references: {
   693→      isAddingToReferences: referencesState.isAddingToReferences,
   694→      addToReferencesSuccess: referencesState.addToReferencesSuccess,
   695→      handleAddToReferences: referencesState.handleAddToReferences,
   696→      isAddingToJoin: joinState.isAddingToJoin,
   697→      addToJoinSuccess: joinState.addToJoinSuccess,
   698→      handleAddToJoin: joinState.handleAddToJoin,
   699→      handleGoToJoin: joinState.handleGoToJoin,
   700→    },
   701→    lineage: {
   702→      derivedItems: lineageState.derivedItems || [],
   703→      derivedGenerations: lineageState.derivedGenerations || [],
   704→      derivedPage: lineageState.derivedPage,
   705→      derivedTotalPages: lineageState.derivedTotalPages,
   706→      paginatedDerived: lineageState.paginatedDerived || [],
   707→      setDerivedPage: lineageState.setDerivedPage,
   708→    },
   709→    shots,
   710→    sourceGeneration: {
   711→      data: sourceGenerationData,
   712→      primaryVariant: sourcePrimaryVariant,
   713→    },
   714→    makeMainVariant,
   715→  };
   716→}
   717→
   718→export interface SharedLightboxPresentationState {
   719→  variants: UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['variants'];
   720→  intendedActiveVariantIdRef: UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['intendedActiveVariantIdRef'];
   721→  navigation: UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['navigation'];
   722→  effectiveMedia: UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['effectiveMedia'];
   723→  layout: UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['layout'];
   724→  buttonGroupProps: UseSharedLightboxStateReturn['buttonGroupProps'];
   725→}
   726→
   727→export function useLightboxPanelModel(
   728→  input: UseSharedLightboxStateInput,
   729→  variantsState: SharedVariantsStateResult,
   730→  interactionState: SharedLightboxInteractionState,
   731→): SharedLightboxPresentationState {
   732→  const navigation = useLightboxNavigationModel(input);
   733→  const { effectiveVideoUrl, effectiveMediaUrl, effectiveImageDimensions } = useEffectiveMedia({
   734→    isVideo: input.core.isVideo,
   735→    activeVariant: variantsState.activeVariant,
   736→    effectiveImageUrl: input.media.effectiveImageUrl,
   737→    imageDimensions: input.media.imageDimensions,
   738→    projectAspectRatio: input.media.projectAspectRatio,
   739→  });
   740→  const layout = useLayoutMode({
   741→    isMobile: input.core.isMobile,
   742→    showTaskDetails: input.layout.showTaskDetails ?? false,
   743→    isSpecialEditMode: input.layout.isSpecialEditMode,
   744→    isVideo: input.core.isVideo,
   745→    isInpaintMode: input.layout.isInpaintMode ?? false,
   746→    isMagicEditMode: input.layout.isMagicEditMode ?? false,
   747→  });
   748→  const buttonGroupProps = useSharedButtonGroupState({
   749→    input,
   750→    localStarred: interactionState.star.localStarred,
   751→    handleToggleStar: interactionState.star.handleToggleStar,
   752→    toggleStarPending: interactionState.star.toggleStarMutation.isPending,
   753→    isAddingToReferences: interactionState.references.isAddingToReferences,
   754→    addToReferencesSuccess: interactionState.references.addToReferencesSuccess,
   755→    handleAddToReferences: interactionState.references.handleAddToReferences,
   756→    handleAddToJoin: interactionState.references.handleAddToJoin,
   757→    isAddingToJoin: interactionState.references.isAddingToJoin,
   758→    addToJoinSuccess: interactionState.references.addToJoinSuccess,
   759→    handleGoToJoin: interactionState.references.handleGoToJoin,
   760→  });
   761→
   762→  return {
   763→    variants: variantsState.section,
   764→    intendedActiveVariantIdRef: variantsState.intendedActiveVariantIdRef,
   765→    navigation,
   766→    effectiveMedia: {
   767→      videoUrl: effectiveVideoUrl,
   768→      mediaUrl: effectiveMediaUrl,
   769→      imageDimensions: effectiveImageDimensions,
   770→    },
   771→    layout: {
   772→      isTabletOrLarger: layout.isTabletOrLarger,
   773→      isTouchLikeDevice: layout.isTouchLikeDevice,
   774→      shouldShowSidePanel: layout.shouldShowSidePanel,
   775→      isUnifiedEditMode: layout.isUnifiedEditMode,
   776→      isPortraitMode: layout.isPortraitMode,
   777→    },
   778→    buttonGroupProps,
   779→  };
   780→}
   781→export const useSharedLightboxPresentationState = useLightboxPanelModel;
   782→

    45→}
    46→
    47→interface UseMagicEditModeReturn {
    48→  isMagicEditMode: boolean;
    49→  setIsMagicEditMode: (value: boolean) => void;
    50→  magicEditPrompt: string;
    51→  setMagicEditPrompt: (value: string) => void;
    52→  magicEditNumImages: number;
    53→  setMagicEditNumImages: (value: number) => void;
    54→  isCreatingMagicEditTasks: boolean;
    55→  magicEditTasksCreated: boolean;
    56→  inpaintPanelPosition: 'left' | 'right';
    57→  setInpaintPanelPosition: (value: 'left' | 'right') => void;
    58→  handleEnterMagicEditMode: () => void;
    59→  handleExitMagicEditMode: () => void;
    60→  handleUnifiedGenerate: () => Promise<void>;
    61→  isSpecialEditMode: boolean;
    62→}
    63→
    64→/**
    65→ * Hook to manage Magic Edit mode state and unified generate handler
    66→ * Handles auto-enter, prompt persistence, and routing between inpaint/magic edit
    67→ */
    68→export const useMagicEditMode = ({
    69→  media,
    70→  selectedProjectId,
    71→  autoEnterInpaint,
    72→  isVideo,
    73→  isInpaintMode,
    74→  setIsInpaintMode,
    75→  handleEnterInpaintMode,
    76→  handleGenerateInpaint,
    77→  brushStrokes,
    78→  inpaintPrompt,
    79→  setInpaintPrompt,
    80→  inpaintNumGenerations,
    81→  setInpaintNumGenerations,
    82→  editModeLoRAs,
    83→  sourceUrlForTasks,
    84→  imageDimensions,
    85→  toolTypeOverride,
    86→  isInSceneBoostEnabled,
    87→  setIsInSceneBoostEnabled,
    88→  activeVariantId,
    89→  activeVariantLocation,
    90→  createAsGeneration,
    91→  advancedSettings,
    92→  qwenEditModel,
    93→  enabled = true,
    94→}: UseMagicEditModeParams): UseMagicEditModeReturn => {
    95→  // Magic Edit mode state
    96→  const [isMagicEditMode, setIsMagicEditMode] = useState(false);
    97→  const [magicEditPrompt, setMagicEditPrompt] = useState('');
    98→  const [magicEditNumImages, setMagicEditNumImages] = useState(4);
    99→  const [isCreatingMagicEditTasks, setIsCreatingMagicEditTasks] = useState(false);
   100→  const [magicEditTasksCreated, setMagicEditTasksCreated] = useState(false);
   101→  const [inpaintPanelPosition, setInpaintPanelPosition] = useState<'left' | 'right'>('right');
   102→
   103→  const { currentShotId } = useCurrentShot();
   104→  const run = useTaskPlaceholder();
   105→
   106→  // Prompt persistence for magic edit mode
   107→  const {
   108→    addMagicEditPrompt,
   109→    getLastMagicEditPrompt,
   110→    getLastSettings,
   111→    isLoading: isLoadingMetadata
   112→  } = useShotGenerationMetadata({
   113→    shotId: currentShotId || '',
   114→    shotGenerationId: media.id,
   115→    enabled: !!(currentShotId && media.id) && enabled !== false
   116→  });
   117→
   118→  // Track if user has manually exited edit mode to prevent auto-re-enter
   119→  const hasManuallyExitedRef = useRef(false);
   120→  // Guard against double-entry during async state updates
   121→  const isEnteringEditModeRef = useRef(false);
   122→  // Track if we've already restored the prompt for this mode entry (prevents re-restore on clear)
   123→  const hasRestoredPromptRef = useRef(false);
   124→
   125→  // Reset flags when media changes
   126→  useEffect(() => {
   127→    hasManuallyExitedRef.current = false;
   128→    isEnteringEditModeRef.current = false;
   129→    hasRestoredPromptRef.current = false;
   130→  }, [media.id]);
   131→
   132→  const handleEnterMagicEditMode = useCallback(() => {
   133→    // Prevent double-entry while state is updating
   134→    if (isEnteringEditModeRef.current) {
   135→      return;
   136→    }
   137→    isEnteringEditModeRef.current = true;
   138→
   139→    setIsMagicEditMode(true);
   140→    handleEnterInpaintMode();
   141→  }, [handleEnterInpaintMode]);
   142→
   143→  const handleExitMagicEditMode = useCallback(() => {
   144→    hasManuallyExitedRef.current = true;
   145→    hasRestoredPromptRef.current = false; // Reset so re-entering can restore again
   146→    setIsMagicEditMode(false);
   147→    setIsInpaintMode(false);
   148→  }, [setIsInpaintMode]);
   149→
   150→  // Auto-enter unified edit mode if requested (only once, not after manual exit)
   151→  useEffect(() => {
   152→    // Reset entry guard once we're actually in edit mode
   153→    if (isInpaintMode || isMagicEditMode) {
   154→      isEnteringEditModeRef.current = false;
   155→      return;
   156→    }
   157→
   158→    if (autoEnterInpaint && !isVideo && selectedProjectId && !hasManuallyExitedRef.current) {
   159→      handleEnterMagicEditMode();
   160→    }
   161→  }, [autoEnterInpaint, isInpaintMode, isMagicEditMode, isVideo, selectedProjectId, handleEnterMagicEditMode]);
   162→
   163→  // Load saved prompt and settings when entering magic edit mode (without brush strokes)
   164→  // Only restore once per mode entry to prevent re-restoring when user clears the prompt
   165→  useEffect(() => {
   166→    if (isMagicEditMode && !isLoadingMetadata && currentShotId && brushStrokes.length === 0 && !hasRestoredPromptRef.current) {
   167→      // Mark as initialized FIRST - we only get one chance to restore per mode entry
   168→      // This prevents clearing the prompt from triggering restoration
   169→      hasRestoredPromptRef.current = true;
   170→
   171→      const lastPrompt = getLastMagicEditPrompt();
   172→      const lastSettings = getLastSettings();
   173→
   174→      if (lastPrompt && !inpaintPrompt) {
   175→        setInpaintPrompt(lastPrompt);
   176→        setInpaintNumGenerations(lastSettings.numImages);
   177→        setIsInSceneBoostEnabled(lastSettings.isInSceneBoostEnabled);
   178→      }
   179→    }
   180→  }, [isMagicEditMode, isLoadingMetadata, currentShotId, brushStrokes.length, getLastMagicEditPrompt, getLastSettings, inpaintPrompt, setInpaintPrompt, setInpaintNumGenerations, setIsInSceneBoostEnabled]);
   181→
   182→  // Unified edit mode - merging inpaint and magic edit
   183→  const isSpecialEditMode = isInpaintMode || isMagicEditMode;
   184→
   185→  // Unified generate handler - routes based on brush strokes
   186→  const handleUnifiedGenerate = useCallback(async () => {
   187→    if (!selectedProjectId) {
   188→      toast.error('No project selected');
   189→      return;
   190→    }
   191→    
   192→    const prompt = inpaintPrompt.trim();
   193→    if (!prompt) {
   194→      toast.error('Please enter a prompt');
   195→      return;
   196→    }
   197→    
   198→    // Route based on whether there are brush strokes
   199→    if (brushStrokes.length > 0) {
   200→      // Has brush strokes -> inpaint
   201→      await handleGenerateInpaint();
   202→    } else {
   203→      // No brush strokes -> magic edit
   204→      setIsCreatingMagicEditTasks(true);
   205→      setMagicEditTasksCreated(false);
   206→
   207→      try {
   208→        await run({
   209→          taskType: 'qwen_image_edit',
   210→          label: prompt || 'Magic edit...',
   211→          context: 'useMagicEditMode',
   212→          toastTitle: 'Failed to create magic edit tasks',
   213→          create: () => {
   214→            // Use active variant's location if viewing a non-primary variant
   215→            const effectiveImageUrl = activeVariantLocation || sourceUrlForTasks;
   216→
   217→            // IMPORTANT: Use generation_id (actual generations.id) when available, falling back to id
   218→            // For ShotImageManager/Timeline images, id is shot_generations.id but generation_id is the actual generation ID
   219→            const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media);
   220→
   221→            return createBatchMagicEditTasks({
   222→              project_id: selectedProjectId,
   223→              prompt,
   224→              image_url: effectiveImageUrl,
   225→              numImages: inpaintNumGenerations,
   226→              negative_prompt: "",
   227→              resolution: imageDimensions ? `${imageDimensions.width}x${imageDimensions.height}` : undefined,
   228→              seed: 11111,
   229→              shot_id: currentShotId || undefined,
   230→              tool_type: toolTypeOverride,
   231→              loras: editModeLoRAs,
   232→              based_on: actualGenerationId ?? undefined,
   233→              source_variant_id: activeVariantId || undefined,
   234→              create_as_generation: createAsGeneration,
   235→              hires_fix: convertToHiresFixApiParams(advancedSettings),
   236→              qwen_edit_model: qwenEditModel,
   237→            });
   238→          },
   239→          onSuccess: async () => {
   240→            // Save the prompt to shot generation metadata
   241→            if (currentShotId && media.id) {
   242→              try {
   243→                await addMagicEditPrompt(
   244→                  prompt,
   245→                  inpaintNumGenerations,
   246→                  false, // Legacy parameter
   247→                  isInSceneBoostEnabled
   248→                );
   249→              } catch (error) {
   250→                normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useMagicEditMode', showToast: false });
```

   190→      icon: React.createElement(Type),
   191→      onClick: () => { setIsInpaintMode(true); setEditMode('text'); },
   192→    },

   194→      id: 'inpaint',
   195→      label: 'Paint',
   196→      icon: React.createElement(Paintbrush),
   197→      onClick: () => { setIsInpaintMode(true); setEditMode('inpaint'); },
   198→    },
   199→    {

   201→      label: 'Annotate',

    91→/**
    92→ * Unified edit settings persistence hook
    93→ *
    94→ * Coordinates:
    95→ * 1. Per-generation settings (generations.params.ui.editSettings)
    96→ * 2. "Last used" settings (useToolSettings + localStorage)
    97→ *
    98→ * Loading behavior:
    99→ * - If generation has persisted settings → use those (including prompt)
   100→ * - If no persisted settings → use "last used" (prompt = '')
   101→ *
   102→ * Saving behavior:
   103→ * - All changes save to generation
   104→ * - Non-prompt changes also update "last used"
   105→ */
   106→export function useEditSettingsPersistence({
   107→  generationId,
   108→  projectId,
   109→  enabled = true,
   110→}: UseEditSettingsPersistenceProps): UseEditSettingsPersistenceReturn {
   111→  // Per-generation settings
   112→  const generationSettings = useGenerationEditSettings({
   113→    generationId,
   114→    enabled,
   115→  });
   116→
   117→  // "Last used" settings
   118→  const lastUsedSettings = useLastUsedEditSettings({
   119→    projectId,
   120→    enabled,
   121→  });
   122→
   123→  // Track initialization state
   124→  const hasInitializedRef = useRef(false);
   125→  const lastGenerationIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
   126→  const [isReady, setIsReady] = useState(false);
   127→
   128→  // Reset initialization on generation change
   129→  useEffect(() => {
   130→    if (generationId !== lastGenerationIdRef.current) {
   131→      hasInitializedRef.current = false;
   132→      lastGenerationIdRef.current = generationId;
   133→      setIsReady(false);
   134→    }
   135→  }, [generationId]);
   136→
   137→  // Extract stable references for the initialization effect
   138→  const { isLoading: genIsLoading, hasPersistedSettings, initializeFromLastUsed } = generationSettings;
   139→  const { lastUsed } = lastUsedSettings;
   140→
   141→  // Initialize from "last used" when generation loads without persisted settings
   142→  useEffect(() => {
   143→    if (!genIsLoading && !hasInitializedRef.current && !hasPersistedSettings && generationId) {
   144→      hasInitializedRef.current = true;
   145→      // Apply "last used" settings (without prompt)
   146→      initializeFromLastUsed(lastUsed);
   147→      setIsReady(true);
   148→    } else if (!genIsLoading && !hasInitializedRef.current && hasPersistedSettings) {
   149→      hasInitializedRef.current = true;
   150→      setIsReady(true);
```

## Image Edit Mode Selection Flow - Comprehensive Analysis

I've thoroughly investigated the image edit mode selection flow in the lightbox. Here's what I found:

### 1. **The "Select an option to start editing" Message**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/edit-images/components/InlineEditView.tsx` (lines 227-245)

This message appears in the **InlineEditSidebar** component when `isSpecialEditMode` is **false**:

```tsx
function InlineEditSidebar({ variant, model, onClose, onNavigateToGeneration }: InlineEditSidebarProps) {
  if (model.isSpecialEditMode) {
    return (
      <EditModePanel
        // ... edit panel shown when in edit mode
      />
    );
  }

  // Fallback: Show mode selector buttons
  return (
    <div className="w-full h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center gap-y-4">
      <h3 className="text-xl font-medium">Image Editor</h3>
      <p className="text-muted-foreground">Select an option to start editing</p>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 w-full max-w-xs">
        <Button onClick={() => {
          model.actions.enterInpaintMode();  // Sets isInpaintMode=true, editMode='inpaint'
        }} className="w-full">
          Inpaint / Erase
        </Button>
        <Button onClick={model.actions.enterMagicEditMode} variant="secondary" className="w-full">
          Magic Edit
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

---

### 2. **The ModeSelector Component**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/components/ModeSelector.tsx`

This is a **responsive mode selector** that:
- Automatically switches between text+icon and icon-only mode based on available width
- Uses a ResizeObserver to detect space constraints
- Shows 6 edit mode options (Text, Paint, Annotate, Move, Img2Img, Enhance)
- Is displayed **only when in edit mode** (`isSpecialEditMode === true`)

```tsx

  className,
}) => {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [showIconsOnly, setShowIconsOnly] = useState(false);
  // ... responsive measurement logic ...
}
```

---

### 3. **Edit Mode State Initialization**

The initialization happens at multiple levels:

#### **Level 1: ImageLightbox Props** 
**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/ImageLightbox.tsx` (line 338)

```tsx
const autoEnterInpaint = props.features?.autoEnterInpaint ?? false;
```

This flag controls whether edit mode is automatically entered when the lightbox opens.

#### **Level 2: useImageEditOrchestrator Hook**
**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useImageEditOrchestrator.ts`

This orchestrator composes all image edit hooks and passes `autoEnterInpaint` to `useMagicEditMode`.

#### **Level 3: useMagicEditMode Hook**
**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useMagicEditMode.ts` (lines 150-161)

```tsx
// Auto-enter unified edit mode if requested (only once, not after manual exit)
useEffect(() => {
  // Reset entry guard once we're actually in edit mode
  if (isInpaintMode || isMagicEditMode) {
    isEnteringEditModeRef.current = false;
    return;
  }

  if (autoEnterInpaint && !isVideo && selectedProjectId && !hasManuallyExitedRef.current) {
    handleEnterMagicEditMode();
  }
}, [autoEnterInpaint, isInpaintMode, isMagicEditMode, isVideo, selectedProjectId, handleEnterMagicEditMode]);
```

The hook tracks:
- `hasManuallyExitedRef` - prevents re-entering after user manually exits
- `isEnteringEditModeRef` - prevents double-entry during state updates
- `hasRestoredPromptRef` - prevents re-restoring saved prompt

### 4. **Key State Flags**

#### **isSpecialEditMode** (the main control flag)
**Definition:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useMagicEditMode.ts` (line 183)

```tsx
const isSpecialEditMode = isInpaintMode || isMagicEditMode;
```

This determines whether the EditModePanel or fallback message is shown.

- When `isSpecialEditMode === true` → **EditModePanel is shown** with ModeSelector
- When `isSpecialEditMode === false` → **Fallback message "Select an option to start editing"** is shown

#### **editMode** (the currently selected mode)
**Type:** `'text' | 'inpaint' | 'annotate' | 'reposition' | 'img2img' | 'upscale'`

Determines which form/controls are displayed within EditModePanel.

### 5. **usePanelModeRestore Hook**

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/usePanelModeRestore.ts`

This hook **automatically restores the last used panel mode** when opening a lightbox:

```tsx
export function usePanelModeRestore({
  mediaId,
  persistedPanelMode,  // 'edit' | 'info' | null
  isSpecialEditMode,
  isInVideoEditMode,
  autoEnterInpaint,
  ...modeHandlers
}: UseVideoPanelModeRestoreProps | UseImagePanelModeRestoreProps): UsePanelModeRestoreReturn {
  
  useEffect(() => {
    if (hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current) return;
    
    // Don't restore if explicit modes are set
    if (initialVideoTrimMode || autoEnterInpaint) {
      hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current = true;
      return;
    }

    // Don't restore if already in edit mode
    if (isSpecialEditMode || isInVideoEditMode) {
      hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current = true;
      return;
    }

    // If last panel mode was 'edit', re-enter edit mode
    if (persistedPanelMode === 'edit') {
      hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current = true;
      setTimeout(() => enterEditMode(), 0);
    }
  }, [...]);
}
```

**Called from:** `ImageLightbox.tsx` (lines 410-419)

### 9. **To Jump Straight into a Mode**

To make the lightbox open directly in a specific edit mode instead of showing the "Select an option" screen:

**Option A: Use autoEnterInpaint flag** (enters magic edit/inpaint unified mode)
- Pass `features={{ autoEnterInpaint: true }}` to ImageLightbox
- The hook `useMagicEditMode` auto-enters edit mode on first render

**Option B: Use usePanelModeRestore** (restores last used mode)
- Call `setPanelMode('edit')` in `useEditSettingsPersistence`
- Next time that media/project is opened, it auto-restores to edit mode

**Option C: Modify mode selector UI** (show selector immediately)
- Change EditPanelLayout to show ModeSelector when `isSpecialEditMode === false`
- Currently ModeSelector only appears after entering a mode

   403→            let hasPrevious: boolean;

   409→                hasPrevious = false;

   427→            // For non-external gens (images from the shot itself), check if they have a timeline_frame
   428→            // Use lightboxSelectedShotId instead of props.selectedShotId so it updates when dropdown changes
   429→            const effectiveSelectedShotId = lightboxSelectedShotId || props.selectedShotId;

   435→            );

   437→            const positionedInSelectedShot = isInSelectedShot

   442→              ? currentImage.timeline_frame === null || currentImage.timeline_frame === undefined
   443→              : undefined;

    79→/** Bundle flat MediaLightboxProps into the grouped sub-interfaces shared by Image/VideoLightbox. */
    80→function useBundledLightboxProps(props: MediaLightboxProps) {
    81→  const navigation: LightboxNavigationProps = useMemo(() => ({
    82→    onNext: props.onNext,
    83→    onPrevious: props.onPrevious,
    84→    showNavigation: props.showNavigation,

    97→    onShowTick: props.onShowTick,
    98→    onShowSecondaryTick: props.onShowSecondaryTick,
    99→    onOptimisticPositioned: props.onOptimisticPositioned,
   100→    onOptimisticUnpositioned: props.onOptimisticUnpositioned,
   101→    optimisticPositionedIds: props.optimisticPositionedIds,
   102→    optimisticUnpositionedIds: props.optimisticUnpositionedIds,
   103→    positionedInSelectedShot: props.positionedInSelectedShot,
   104→    associatedWithoutPositionInSelectedShot: props.associatedWithoutPositionInSelectedShot,
   105→  }), [
   106→    props.allShots, props.selectedShotId, props.onShotChange,
   107→    props.onAddToShot, props.onAddToShotWithoutPosition,
   108→    props.onCreateShot, props.onNavigateToShot,
   109→    props.onShowTick, props.onShowSecondaryTick,
   110→    props.onOptimisticPositioned, props.onOptimisticUnpositioned,
   111→    props.optimisticPositionedIds, props.optimisticUnpositionedIds,
   112→    props.positionedInSelectedShot, props.associatedWithoutPositionInSelectedShot,
   113→  ]);
   114→
   115→  const features: LightboxFeatureFlags = useMemo(() => ({
   116→    showImageEditTools: props.showImageEditTools,
   117→    showDownload: props.showDownload,
   118→    showMagicEdit: props.showMagicEdit,
   119→    autoEnterInpaint: props.autoEnterInpaint,

   121→  }), [props.showImageEditTools, props.showDownload, props.showMagicEdit, props.autoEnterInpaint, props.showTaskDetails]);

   130→

   146→  return { navigation, shotWorkflow, features, actions, videoProps };
   147→}
   148→
   149→const MediaLightbox: React.FC<MediaLightboxProps> = (props) => {
   150→  const { media, segmentSlotMode } = props;
   151→
   152→  // Bundle flat props into grouped sub-interfaces (hook must be called unconditionally)
   153→  const bundled = useBundledLightboxProps(props);
   154→
   155→  if (segmentSlotMode) {
   156→    return (
   157→      <VideoLightbox
   158→        media={media}
   159→        onClose={props.onClose}
   160→        segmentSlotMode={segmentSlotMode}
   161→        readOnly={props.readOnly}
   162→        shotId={props.shotId}
   163→        initialVariantId={props.initialVariantId}
   164→        taskDetailsData={props.taskDetailsData}
   165→        onOpenExternalGeneration={props.onOpenExternalGeneration}
   166→        showTickForImageId={props.showTickForImageId}
   167→        showTickForSecondaryImageId={props.showTickForSecondaryImageId}
   168→        tasksPaneOpen={props.tasksPaneOpen}
   169→        tasksPaneWidth={props.tasksPaneWidth}
   170→        adjacentSegments={props.adjacentSegments}
   171→        navigation={bundled.navigation}
   172→        shotWorkflow={bundled.shotWorkflow}
   173→        features={bundled.features}
   174→        actions={bundled.actions}
   175→        videoProps={bundled.videoProps}
   176→        parentGenerationIdOverride={props.parentGenerationIdOverride}
   177→      />
   178→    );
   179→  }
   180→
   181→  if (!media) {
   182→    return null;
   183→  }
   184→
   185→  if (isVideoAny(media)) {
   186→    return (
   187→      <VideoLightbox
   188→        media={media}
   189→        onClose={props.onClose}
   190→        readOnly={props.readOnly}
   191→        shotId={props.shotId}
   192→        initialVariantId={props.initialVariantId}
   193→        taskDetailsData={props.taskDetailsData}
   194→        onOpenExternalGeneration={props.onOpenExternalGeneration}
   195→        showTickForImageId={props.showTickForImageId}
   196→        showTickForSecondaryImageId={props.showTickForSecondaryImageId}
   197→        tasksPaneOpen={props.tasksPaneOpen}
   198→        tasksPaneWidth={props.tasksPaneWidth}
   199→        adjacentSegments={props.adjacentSegments}
   200→        navigation={bundled.navigation}
   201→        shotWorkflow={bundled.shotWorkflow}
   202→        features={bundled.features}
   203→        actions={bundled.actions}
   204→        videoProps={bundled.videoProps}
   205→        parentGenerationIdOverride={props.parentGenerationIdOverride}
   206→      />
   207→    );
   208→  }
   209→
   210→  return (
   211→    <ImageLightbox
   212→      media={media}
   213→      onClose={props.onClose}
   214→      readOnly={props.readOnly}
   215→      shotId={props.shotId}
   216→      initialVariantId={props.initialVariantId}
   217→      toolTypeOverride={props.toolTypeOverride}
   218→      taskDetailsData={props.taskDetailsData}
   219→      onOpenExternalGeneration={props.onOpenExternalGeneration}
   220→      onNavigateToGeneration={props.onNavigateToGeneration}
   221→      showTickForImageId={props.showTickForImageId}
   222→      showTickForSecondaryImageId={props.showTickForSecondaryImageId}
   223→      tasksPaneOpen={props.tasksPaneOpen}
   224→      tasksPaneWidth={props.tasksPaneWidth}
   225→      adjacentSegments={props.adjacentSegments}
   226→      navigation={bundled.navigation}
   227→      shotWorkflow={bundled.shotWorkflow}
   228→      features={bundled.features}
   229→      actions={bundled.actions}
   230→    />
   231→  );
   232→};
   233→
   234→export default MediaLightbox;
   235→

    96→function useImageLightboxEnvironment(props: ImageLightboxProps) {

   109→

   116→  const effectiveTasksPaneOpen = tasksPaneOpen ?? tasksPaneOpenContext;

   118→

   120→  const imageContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

   127→  const actualGenerationId = getGenerationId(media);
   128→  const variantFetchGenerationId = media.parent_generation_id || actualGenerationId;
   129→
   130→  const [imageDimensions, setImageDimensions] = useState<{ width: number; height: number } | null>(() => {

   213→  return {

   232→    },
   233→    shots: {

   242→      onShowSecondaryTick: shotWorkflow?.onShowSecondaryTick,
   243→      onOptimisticPositioned: shotWorkflow?.onOptimisticPositioned,
   244→      onOptimisticUnpositioned: shotWorkflow?.onOptimisticUnpositioned,
   245→      optimisticPositionedIds: shotWorkflow?.optimisticPositionedIds,
   246→      optimisticUnpositionedIds: shotWorkflow?.optimisticUnpositionedIds,
   247→      positionedInSelectedShot: shotWorkflow?.positionedInSelectedShot,
   248→      associatedWithoutPositionInSelectedShot: shotWorkflow?.associatedWithoutPositionInSelectedShot,
   249→    },
   250→    layout: {
   251→      showTaskDetails: features?.showTaskDetails,
   252→      isSpecialEditMode: modeSnapshot.isMagicEditMode,
   253→      isInpaintMode: modeSnapshot.isInpaintMode,
   254→      isMagicEditMode: modeSnapshot.isMagicEditMode,

   259→      isDownloading: env.isDownloading,
   260→      setIsDownloading: env.setIsDownloading,
   261→      onDelete: actions?.onDelete,
   262→      isDeleting: actions?.isDeleting,
   263→      isUpscaling: env.upscaleHook.isUpscaling,
   264→      handleUpscale: () => {
   265→        void env.upscaleHook.handleUpscale({ scaleFactor: 2, noiseScale: 0.1 });
   266→      },
   267→    },
   268→    media: {
   269→      effectiveImageUrl: env.upscaleHook.effectiveImageUrl,
   270→      imageDimensions: env.imageDimensions || { width: 1024, height: 1024 },
   271→      projectAspectRatio: env.projectAspectRatio,

   274→    onOpenExternalGeneration: props.onOpenExternalGeneration,

   276→}

   278→function useImageLightboxSharedState(props: ImageLightboxProps, env: ImageLightboxEnvironment) {
   279→  const navigation = props.navigation;
   280→
   281→  const {
   282→    upscaleHook,
   283→  } = env;
   284→
   285→  const handleSlotNavNext = useCallback(() => {
   286→    navigation?.onNext?.();
   287→  }, [navigation]);
   288→
   289→  const handleSlotNavPrev = useCallback(() => {
   290→    navigation?.onPrevious?.();
   291→  }, [navigation]);
   292→
   293→  const [modeSnapshot, setModeSnapshot] = useState({
   294→    isInpaintMode: false,
   295→    isMagicEditMode: false,
   296→  });
   297→
   298→  const sharedInput = buildImageSharedLightboxInput({
   299→    props,
   300→    env,
   301→    modeSnapshot,
   302→    handleSlotNavNext,
   303→    handleSlotNavPrev,
   304→  });
   305→  const sharedState = useSharedLightboxState(sharedInput);
   306→
   307→  useEffect(() => {
   308→    upscaleHook.setActiveVariant(sharedState.variants.activeVariant?.location, sharedState.variants.activeVariant?.id);
   309→  }, [
   310→    sharedState.variants.activeVariant?.location,
   311→    sharedState.variants.activeVariant?.id,
   312→    upscaleHook,
   313→  ]);
   314→
   315→  return {
   316→    sharedState,
   317→    handleSlotNavNext,
   318→    handleSlotNavPrev,
   319→    modeSnapshot,
   320→    setModeSnapshot,
   321→  };
   322→}

   326→function useImageLightboxEditing(
   327→  props: ImageLightboxProps,
   328→  env: ImageLightboxEnvironment,
   329→  sharedModel: ImageLightboxSharedModel,
   330→) {
   331→  const {
   332→    media,
   333→    taskDetailsData,
   334→    initialVariantId,
   335→    toolTypeOverride,
   336→    shotId,
   337→  } = props;
   338→  const autoEnterInpaint = props.features?.autoEnterInpaint ?? false;
   339→
   340→  const {
   341→    selectedProjectId,
   342→    actualGenerationId,
   343→    imageDimensions,
   344→    imageContainerRef,
   345→    editSettingsPersistence,
   346→    effectiveEditModeLoRAs,
   347→    availableLoras,
   348→    variantFetchGenerationId,
   349→  } = env;
   350→
   351→  const {
   352→    sharedState,
   353→    setModeSnapshot,
   354→  } = sharedModel;
   355→
   356→  const editOrchestrator = useImageEditOrchestrator({
   357→    mediaContext: {
   358→      media,
   359→      selectedProjectId,
   360→      actualGenerationId,
   361→      shotId,
   362→      toolTypeOverride,
   363→      autoEnterInpaint,
   364→      thumbnailUrl: sharedState.variants.activeVariant?.thumbnail_url || media.thumbUrl,
   365→    },
   366→    displayContext: {
   367→      imageDimensions,
   368→      imageContainerRef,
   369→      effectiveImageUrl: env.upscaleHook.effectiveImageUrl,
   370→    },
   371→    variantContext: {
   372→      activeVariant: sharedState.variants.activeVariant,
   373→      setActiveVariantId: sharedState.variants.setActiveVariantId,
   374→      refetchVariants: sharedState.variants.refetch,
   375→    },
   376→    settingsContext: editSettingsPersistence,
   377→    loraContext: {
   378→      effectiveEditModeLoRAs,
   379→      availableLoras,
   380→    },
   381→  });
   382→
   383→  useEffect(() => {
   384→    setModeSnapshot((current) => {
   385→      if (
   386→        current.isInpaintMode === editOrchestrator.isInpaintMode
   387→        && current.isMagicEditMode === editOrchestrator.isMagicEditMode
   388→      ) {
   389→        return current;
   390→      }
   391→      return {
   392→        isInpaintMode: editOrchestrator.isInpaintMode,
   393→        isMagicEditMode: editOrchestrator.isMagicEditMode,
   394→      };
   395→    });
   396→  }, [
   397→    editOrchestrator.isInpaintMode,
   398→    editOrchestrator.isMagicEditMode,
   399→    setModeSnapshot,
   400→  ]);
   401→
   402→  const { adjustedTaskDetailsData } = useAdjustedTaskDetails({
   403→    projectId: selectedProjectId ?? null,
   404→    activeVariant: sharedState.variants.activeVariant,
   405→    taskDetailsData,
   406→    isLoadingVariants: sharedState.variants.isLoading,
   407→    initialVariantId,
   408→  });
   409→
   410→  usePanelModeRestore({
   411→    mediaId: media.id,
   412→    persistedPanelMode: editSettingsPersistence.panelMode,
   413→    isVideo: false,
   414→    isSpecialEditMode: editOrchestrator.isSpecialEditMode,
   415→    isInVideoEditMode: false,
   416→    initialVideoTrimMode: false,
   417→    autoEnterInpaint,
   418→    handleEnterMagicEditMode: editOrchestrator.handleEnterMagicEditMode,
   419→  });
   420→
   421→  const variantBadges = useLightboxVariantBadges({
   422→    pendingTaskGenerationId: env.actualGenerationId,
   423→    selectedProjectId,
   424→    variants: sharedState.variants.list,
   425→    variantFetchGenerationId,
   426→  });
   427→
   428→  return {
   429→    editOrchestrator,
   430→    adjustedTaskDetailsData,
   431→    variantBadges,
   432→  };
   433→}

   435→type ImageLightboxEditModel = ReturnType<typeof useImageLightboxEditing>;
   436→
   437→function useImageLightboxControlsPanel(
   438→  props: ImageLightboxProps,
   439→  env: ImageLightboxEnvironment,
   440→  sharedModel: ImageLightboxSharedModel,
   441→  editModel: ImageLightboxEditModel,
   442→  showPanel: boolean,
   443→  panelVariant: 'desktop' | 'mobile',
   444→  panelTaskId: string | null,
   445→) {
   446→  const {
   447→    media,
   448→    shotId,
   449→    onOpenExternalGeneration,
   450→  } = props;
   451→  const allShots = props.shotWorkflow?.allShots;
   452→  const selectedShotId = props.shotWorkflow?.selectedShotId;
   453→  const showImageEditTools = props.features?.showImageEditTools ?? true;
   454→
   455→  const {
   456→    sharedState,
   457→  } = sharedModel;
   458→
   459→  const { editOrchestrator, adjustedTaskDetailsData } = editModel;
   460→  const primaryVariantId = sharedState.variants.primaryVariant?.id;
   461→
   462→  return useMemo(() => {
   463→    if (!showPanel) {
   464→      return undefined;
   465→    }
   466→
   467→    if (editOrchestrator.isSpecialEditMode) {
   468→      return (
   469→        <EditModePanel
   470→          variant={panelVariant}
   471→          sourceGenerationData={sharedState.sourceGeneration.data}
   472→          onOpenExternalGeneration={onOpenExternalGeneration}
   473→          currentShotId={selectedShotId || shotId}
   474→          allShots={allShots || []}
   475→          isCurrentMediaPositioned={sharedState.shots.isAlreadyPositionedInSelectedShot}
   476→          onReplaceInShot={() => Promise.resolve()}
   477→          sourcePrimaryVariant={sharedState.sourceGeneration.primaryVariant}
   478→          onMakeMainVariant={sharedState.makeMainVariant.handle}
   479→          canMakeMainVariant={sharedState.makeMainVariant.canMake}
   480→          taskId={panelTaskId}
   481→          currentMediaId={media.id}
   482→          handleUnifiedGenerate={editOrchestrator.handleUnifiedGenerate}
   483→          handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit={editOrchestrator.handleGenerateAnnotatedEdit}
   484→          handleGenerateReposition={editOrchestrator.handleGenerateReposition}
   485→          handleSaveAsVariant={editOrchestrator.handleSaveAsVariant}
   486→          handleGenerateImg2Img={editOrchestrator.handleGenerateImg2Img}
   487→          isCloudMode={env.isCloudMode}
   488→          handleUpscale={async () => {
   489→            await env.upscaleHook.handleUpscale({ scaleFactor: 2, noiseScale: 0.1 });
   490→          }}
   491→          isUpscaling={env.upscaleHook.isUpscaling}
   492→          upscaleSuccess={env.upscaleHook.upscaleSuccess}
   493→          img2imgLoraManager={editOrchestrator.img2imgLoraManager}
   494→          editLoraManager={env.editLoraManager}
   495→          availableLoras={env.availableLoras}
   496→          advancedSettings={env.editSettingsPersistence.advancedSettings}
   497→          setAdvancedSettings={env.editSettingsPersistence.setAdvancedSettings}
   498→          isLocalGeneration={env.isLocalGeneration}
   499→        />
   500→      );
   501→    }
   502→
   503→    return (
   504→      <InfoPanel
   505→        variant={panelVariant}
   506→        showImageEditTools={showImageEditTools}
   507→        taskDetailsData={adjustedTaskDetailsData}
   508→        derivedItems={sharedState.lineage.derivedItems}
   509→        derivedGenerations={sharedState.lineage.derivedGenerations}
   510→        paginatedDerived={sharedState.lineage.paginatedDerived}
   511→        derivedPage={sharedState.lineage.derivedPage}
   512→        derivedTotalPages={sharedState.lineage.derivedTotalPages}
   513→        onSetDerivedPage={sharedState.lineage.setDerivedPage}
   514→        onNavigateToGeneration={onOpenExternalGeneration}
   515→        currentMediaId={media.id}

   892→
   893→export const ImageLightbox: React.FC<ImageLightboxProps> = (props) => {
   894→  const env = useImageLightboxEnvironment(props);
   895→  const sharedModel = useImageLightboxSharedState(props, env);
   896→  const editModel = useImageLightboxEditing(props, env, sharedModel);
   897→  const renderModel = useImageLightboxRenderModel(props, env, sharedModel, editModel);
   898→  const overlayViewport: OverlayViewportConstraints = {
   899→    tasksPaneOpen: env.effectiveTasksPaneOpen,
   900→    tasksPaneWidth: env.effectiveTasksPaneWidth,
   901→    tasksPaneLocked: env.isTasksPaneLocked,
   902→    isTabletOrLarger: sharedModel.sharedState.layout.isTabletOrLarger,
   903→    needsFullscreenLayout: renderModel.needsFullscreenLayout,
   904→    needsTasksPaneOffset: renderModel.needsTasksPaneOffset,
   905→  };
   906→
   907→  return (
   908→    <LightboxProviders stateValue={renderModel.lightboxStateValue}>
   909→      <ImageEditProvider value={editModel.editOrchestrator.imageEditValue}>
   910→        <LightboxShell
   911→          onClose={props.onClose}
   912→          hasCanvasOverlay={editModel.editOrchestrator.isInpaintMode}
   913→          isRepositionMode={editModel.editOrchestrator.isInpaintMode && editModel.editOrchestrator.editMode === 'reposition'}
   914→          isMobile={env.isMobile}
   915→          overlayViewport={overlayViewport}
   916→          contentRef={env.contentRef}
   917→          accessibilityTitle={renderModel.accessibilityTitle}
   918→          accessibilityDescription={renderModel.accessibilityDescription}
   919→        >
   920→          <LightboxLayout {...renderModel.layoutProps} controlsPanelContent={renderModel.controlsPanelContent} />
   921→        </LightboxShell>
   922→      </ImageEditProvider>
   923→    </LightboxProviders>
   924→  );
   925→};

1→/**
     2→ * usePanelModeRestore Hook
     3→ *
     4→ * Handles automatic restoration of edit/info panel mode when media changes.
     5→ * If the user was last in edit mode, automatically re-enters edit mode.
     6→ */
     7→
     8→import { useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
     9→
    10→interface UsePanelModeRestoreProps {
    11→  /** Current media ID for tracking changes */
    12→  mediaId: string;
    13→  /** Persisted panel mode from last used settings */
    14→  persistedPanelMode: 'edit' | 'info' | null | undefined;
    15→  /** Whether currently in special edit mode (image) */
    16→  isSpecialEditMode: boolean;
    17→  /** Whether currently in video edit mode */
    18→  isInVideoEditMode: boolean;
    19→  /** Whether initial video trim mode was requested */
    20→  initialVideoTrimMode?: boolean;
    21→  /** Whether auto-enter inpaint was requested */
    22→  autoEnterInpaint?: boolean;
    23→}
    24→
    25→interface UseVideoPanelModeRestoreProps extends UsePanelModeRestoreProps {
    26→  isVideo: true;
    27→  /** Handler to enter video edit mode */
    28→  handleEnterVideoEditMode: () => void;
    29→}
    30→
    31→interface UseImagePanelModeRestoreProps extends UsePanelModeRestoreProps {
    32→  isVideo: false;
    33→  /** Handler to enter magic edit mode (image) */
    34→  handleEnterMagicEditMode: () => void;
    35→}
    36→
    37→interface UsePanelModeRestoreReturn {
    38→  /** Whether panel mode has been restored for current media */
    39→  hasRestoredPanelMode: boolean;
    40→}
    41→
    42→/**
    43→ * Automatically restores the last used panel mode (edit/info) when opening a media item.
    44→ * Prevents loops by tracking restoration per-media and respecting explicit modes.
    45→ */
    46→export function usePanelModeRestore({
    47→  mediaId,
    48→  persistedPanelMode,
    49→  isVideo,
    50→  isSpecialEditMode,
    51→  isInVideoEditMode,
    52→  initialVideoTrimMode,
    53→  autoEnterInpaint,
    54→  ...modeHandlers
    55→}: UseVideoPanelModeRestoreProps | UseImagePanelModeRestoreProps): UsePanelModeRestoreReturn {
    56→  const hasRestoredPanelModeRef = useRef(false);
    57→  const enterEditMode = isVideo
    58→    ? modeHandlers.handleEnterVideoEditMode
    59→    : modeHandlers.handleEnterMagicEditMode;
    60→
    61→  // Main restoration effect
    62→  useEffect(() => {
    63→
    64→    // Only restore once per media (prevent loops)
    65→    if (hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current) {
    66→      return;
    67→    }
    68→
    69→    // Don't restore if initialVideoTrimMode or autoEnterInpaint is set (explicit modes take precedence)
    70→    if (initialVideoTrimMode || autoEnterInpaint) {
    71→      hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current = true;
    72→      return;
    73→    }
    74→
    75→    // Don't restore if already in edit mode
    76→    if (isSpecialEditMode || isInVideoEditMode) {
    77→      hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current = true;
    78→      return;
    79→    }
    80→
    81→    if (persistedPanelMode === 'edit') {
    82→      hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current = true;
    83→      setTimeout(() => enterEditMode(), 0);
    84→    } else {
    85→      hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current = true;
    86→    }
    87→  }, [
    88→    persistedPanelMode,
    89→    enterEditMode,
    90→    initialVideoTrimMode,
    91→    autoEnterInpaint,
    92→    isSpecialEditMode,
    93→    isInVideoEditMode,
    94→  ]);
    95→
    96→  // Reset restore flag when media changes
    97→  useEffect(() => {
    98→    hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current = false;
    99→  }, [mediaId]);
   100→
   101→  return {
   102→    hasRestoredPanelMode: hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current,
   103→  };
   104→}
   105→
