1→import { useState, useCallback, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';
     2→import { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
     3→import { DerivedNavContext } from '../types';
     4→
     5→interface UseLightboxProps {
     6→  images: GenerationRow[];
     7→  externalGenerations: GenerationRow[];
     8→  tempDerivedGenerations: GenerationRow[];
     9→  derivedNavContext: DerivedNavContext | null;
    10→  handleOpenExternalGeneration: (generationId: string, derivedContext?: string[]) => Promise<void>;
    11→}
    12→
    13→export function useLightbox({
    14→  images,
    15→  externalGenerations,
    16→  tempDerivedGenerations,
    17→  derivedNavContext,
    18→  handleOpenExternalGeneration
    19→}: UseLightboxProps) {
    20→  const [lightboxIndex, setLightboxIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);
    21→  const [shouldAutoEnterInpaint, setShouldAutoEnterInpaint] = useState(false);
    22→  
    23→  // Combine all images for lightbox navigation
    24→  const currentImages = useMemo(() => {
    25→    const baseImages = (images && images.length > 0) ? images : [];
    26→    return [...baseImages, ...externalGenerations, ...tempDerivedGenerations];
    27→  }, [images, externalGenerations, tempDerivedGenerations]);
    28→  
    29→  // Get current lightbox image
    30→  const currentLightboxImage = lightboxIndex !== null ? currentImages[lightboxIndex] : null;
    31→  
    32→  // Close lightbox if current image no longer exists (e.g., deleted)
    33→  useEffect(() => {
    34→    
    35→    if (lightboxIndex !== null && lightboxIndex >= currentImages.length) {
    36→      setLightboxIndex(null);
    37→      setShouldAutoEnterInpaint(false);
    38→    }
    39→  }, [lightboxIndex, currentImages.length, images.length, externalGenerations.length, tempDerivedGenerations.length, derivedNavContext]);
    40→  
    41→  // Handle next navigation
    42→  const handleNext = useCallback(() => {
    43→    if (lightboxIndex === null) return;
    44→    
    45→    if (derivedNavContext) {
    46→      const currentId = currentImages[lightboxIndex]?.id;
    47→      const currentDerivedIndex = derivedNavContext.derivedGenerationIds.indexOf(currentId);
    48→      
    49→      if (currentDerivedIndex !== -1 && currentDerivedIndex < derivedNavContext.derivedGenerationIds.length - 1) {
    50→        const nextId = derivedNavContext.derivedGenerationIds[currentDerivedIndex + 1];
    51→        handleOpenExternalGeneration(nextId, derivedNavContext.derivedGenerationIds);
    52→      }
    53→    } else {
    54→      if (lightboxIndex < currentImages.length - 1) {
    55→        setLightboxIndex(lightboxIndex + 1);
    56→      }
    57→    }
    58→  }, [lightboxIndex, currentImages, derivedNavContext, handleOpenExternalGeneration]);
    59→  
    60→  // Handle previous navigation
    61→  const handlePrevious = useCallback(() => {
    62→    if (lightboxIndex === null) return;
    63→    
    64→    if (derivedNavContext) {
    65→      const currentId = currentImages[lightboxIndex]?.id;
    66→      const currentDerivedIndex = derivedNavContext.derivedGenerationIds.indexOf(currentId);
    67→      
    68→      if (currentDerivedIndex !== -1 && currentDerivedIndex > 0) {
    69→        const prevId = derivedNavContext.derivedGenerationIds[currentDerivedIndex - 1];
    70→        handleOpenExternalGeneration(prevId, derivedNavContext.derivedGenerationIds);
    71→      }
    72→    } else {
    73→      if (lightboxIndex > 0) {
    74→        setLightboxIndex(lightboxIndex - 1);
    75→      }
    76→    }
    77→  }, [lightboxIndex, currentImages, derivedNavContext, handleOpenExternalGeneration]);
    78→  
    79→  return {
    80→    lightboxIndex,
    81→    setLightboxIndex,
    82→    shouldAutoEnterInpaint,
    83→    setShouldAutoEnterInpaint,
    84→    currentImages,
    85→    currentLightboxImage,
    86→    handleNext,
    87→    handlePrevious
    88→  };
    89→}
    90→

1→import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
     2→import { GenerationRow } from '@/domains/generation/types';
     3→
     4→interface LightboxProps {
     5→  images: GenerationRow[];
     6→  shotId: string;
     7→  isMobile?: boolean;
     8→}
     9→
    10→export function useLightbox({ images, isMobile = false }: LightboxProps) {
    11→  
    12→  // Lightbox state
    13→  const [lightboxIndex, setLightboxIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);
    14→  const [autoEnterInpaint, setAutoEnterInpaint] = useState(false);
    15→
    16→  // Navigation functions
    17→  const goNext = useCallback(() => {
    18→    setLightboxIndex(i => {
    19→      if (i === null) return null;
    20→      return (i + 1) % images.length;
    21→    });
    22→  }, [images.length]);
    23→
    24→  const goPrev = useCallback(() => {
    25→    setLightboxIndex(i => {
    26→      if (i === null) return null;
    27→      return (i - 1 + images.length) % images.length;
    28→    });
    29→  }, [images.length]);
    30→
    31→  const openLightbox = useCallback((index: number) => {
    32→    if (index >= 0 && index < images.length) {
    33→      setLightboxIndex(index);
    34→    }
    35→  }, [images]);
    36→
    37→  const openLightboxWithInpaint = useCallback((index: number) => {
    38→    if (index >= 0 && index < images.length) {
    39→      setAutoEnterInpaint(true);
    40→      setLightboxIndex(index);
    41→    }
    42→  }, [images]);
    43→
    44→  const closeLightbox = useCallback(() => {
    45→    setLightboxIndex(null);
    46→    setAutoEnterInpaint(false); // Reset auto-enter flag when closing
    47→  }, []);
    48→
    49→  // Handle mobile tap - called by the "Open" button in TimelineItem when selected
    50→  const handleMobileTap = useCallback((idx: number) => {
    51→    if (!isMobile) return;
    52→    openLightbox(idx);
    53→  }, [isMobile, openLightbox]);
    54→
    55→  // Desktop double-click handler
    56→  const handleDesktopDoubleClick = useCallback((idx: number) => {
    57→    if (isMobile) return;
    58→    openLightbox(idx);
    59→  }, [isMobile, openLightbox]);
    60→
    61→  // Get current lightbox image
    62→  const currentLightboxImage = lightboxIndex !== null ? images[lightboxIndex] : null;
    63→
    64→  // Navigation availability
    65→  const hasNext = lightboxIndex !== null && lightboxIndex < images.length - 1;
    66→  const hasPrevious = lightboxIndex !== null && lightboxIndex > 0;
    67→  const showNavigation = images.length > 1;
    68→
    69→  return {
    70→    // State
    71→    lightboxIndex,
    72→    setLightboxIndex, // Expose raw setter for external hooks
    73→    currentLightboxImage,
    74→    autoEnterInpaint,
    75→    
    76→    // Navigation
    77→    goNext,
    78→    goPrev,
    79→    openLightbox,
    80→    openLightboxWithInpaint,
    81→    closeLightbox,
    82→    
    83→    // Event handlers
    84→    handleMobileTap,
    85→    handleDesktopDoubleClick,
    86→    
    87→    // Navigation state
    88→    hasNext,
    89→    hasPrevious,
    90→    showNavigation
    91→  };
    92→}
    93→

    46→
    47→interface UseMediaGalleryActionsReturn {
    48→  handleOptimisticDelete: (imageId: string) => Promise<void>;
    49→  handleOpenLightbox: (image: GeneratedImageWithMetadata) => void;
    50→  handleCloseLightbox: () => void;
    51→  handleDownloadImage: (rawUrl: string, filename: string, imageId?: string, isVideo?: boolean, originalContentType?: string) => Promise<void>;
    52→  handleDownloadStarred: () => Promise<void>;
    53→  handleShowTick: (imageId: string) => void;
    54→  handleShowSecondaryTick: (imageId: string) => void;
    55→  handleShotChange: (shotId: string) => void;
    56→}
    57→
    58→export const useMediaGalleryActions = ({
    59→  onDelete,
    60→  activeLightboxMedia,
    61→  setActiveLightboxMedia,
    62→  setAutoEnterEditMode,
    63→  markOptimisticDeleted,
    64→  markOptimisticDeletedWithBackfill,
    65→  removeOptimisticDeleted,
    66→  setDownloadingImageId,
    67→  setShowTickForImageId,
    68→  setShowTickForSecondaryImageId,
    69→  mainTickTimeoutRef,
    70→  secondaryTickTimeoutRef,
    71→  onBackfillRequest,
    72→  serverPage,
    73→  itemsPerPage,
    74→  isServerPagination,
    75→  setIsBackfillLoading,
    76→  filteredImages,
    77→  setIsDownloadingStarred,
    78→  setSelectedShotIdLocal,
    79→  totalCount,
    80→  onPageBoundsExceeded,
    81→}: UseMediaGalleryActionsProps): UseMediaGalleryActionsReturn => {
    82→
    83→  const { setLastAffectedShotId } = useLastAffectedShot();
    84→
    85→  // Track in-flight delete operations for debouncing rapid deletions
    86→  const pendingDeletesRef = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
    87→  const refetchTimeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);
    88→  const clearLoadingTimeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);
    89→
    90→  /**
    91→   * Optimistic delete with skeleton placeholder at end of grid.
    92→   *
    93→   * Flow:
    94→   * 1. Mark item as deleted (item disappears, others shift)
    95→   * 2. Show skeleton at end of grid while waiting for backfill
    96→   * 3. Call server delete
    97→   * 4. On success: trigger immediate refetch (debounced 100ms for rapid deletes)
    98→   * 5. On failure: revert optimistic state, hide skeleton, show error
    99→   * 6. Check page bounds after refetch
   100→   */
   101→  const handleOptimisticDelete = useCallback(async (imageId: string) => {
   102→    // Use combined action for atomic state update - both deleted mark AND backfill loading
   103→    // in a single dispatch, ensuring skeleton appears in same render as item disappears
   104→    if (isServerPagination) {
   105→      markOptimisticDeletedWithBackfill(imageId);
   106→    } else {
   107→      markOptimisticDeleted(imageId);
   108→    }
   109→    pendingDeletesRef.current.add(imageId);
   110→
   111→    // 3. Close lightbox if this image is open
   112→    if (activeLightboxMedia?.id === imageId) {
   113→      setActiveLightboxMedia(null);
   114→    }
   115→
   116→    try {
   117→      // 4. Delete on server
   118→      if (onDelete) {
   119→        await onDelete(imageId);
   120→      }
   121→
   122→      // 5. Trigger refetch (debounced 100ms to batch rapid deletions)
   123→      if (isServerPagination && onBackfillRequest) {
   124→        // Clear existing timeouts (debounce and any pending clear)
   125→        if (refetchTimeoutRef.current) {
   126→          clearTimeout(refetchTimeoutRef.current);
   127→        }
   128→        if (clearLoadingTimeoutRef.current) {
   129→          clearTimeout(clearLoadingTimeoutRef.current);
   130→        }
   131→
   132→        // Short debounce to batch rapid deletions
   133→        refetchTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(async () => {
   134→
   135→          try {
   136→            await onBackfillRequest();
   137→
   138→            // Check page bounds after refetch
   139→            const newLastPage = computeBackfillLastPage(
   140→              totalCount,
   141→              pendingDeletesRef.current.size,
   142→              itemsPerPage,
   143→            );
   144→
   145→            if (serverPage && serverPage > newLastPage) {
   146→              onPageBoundsExceeded?.(newLastPage);
   147→            }
   148→
   149→            // Clear backfill loading after a short delay to allow image to load
   150→            // The skeleton stays visible during this time, then fades out
   151→            // Use ref so this can be cancelled if more deletes happen
   152→            clearLoadingTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   153→              setIsBackfillLoading(false);
   154→              clearLoadingTimeoutRef.current = null;
   155→            }, INTERACTION_TIMING.galleryBackfillLoadingClearMs);
   156→          } catch (error) {
   157→            normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useMediaGalleryActions', showToast: false });
   158→            setIsBackfillLoading(false);
   159→          }
   160→          // Clear pending deletes after refetch attempt
   161→          pendingDeletesRef.current.clear();
   162→        }, INTERACTION_TIMING.galleryDeleteRefetchDebounceMs);
   163→      }
   164→
   165→    } catch (error) {
   166→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useMediaGalleryActions', toastTitle: 'Delete Failed' });
   167→
   168→      // Revert optimistic state
   169→      removeOptimisticDeleted(imageId);
   170→      pendingDeletesRef.current.delete(imageId);
   171→
   172→      // Hide skeleton if no more pending deletes
   173→      if (pendingDeletesRef.current.size === 0) {
   174→        setIsBackfillLoading(false);
   175→      }
   176→    }
   177→  }, [
   178→    markOptimisticDeleted,
   179→    markOptimisticDeletedWithBackfill,
   180→    removeOptimisticDeleted,
   181→    onDelete,
   182→    activeLightboxMedia,
   183→    setActiveLightboxMedia,
   184→    isServerPagination,
   185→    serverPage,
   186→    onBackfillRequest,
   187→    itemsPerPage,
   188→    setIsBackfillLoading,
   189→    totalCount,
   190→    onPageBoundsExceeded
   191→  ]);
   192→
   193→  const handleOpenLightbox = useCallback((image: GeneratedImageWithMetadata, autoEnterEditMode = false) => {
   194→    const generationId = getGenerationId(image);
   195→
   196→    setActiveLightboxMedia({
   197→      ...image,
   198→      generation_id: image.generation_id ?? generationId ?? undefined,
   199→      location: image.location ?? image.url,
   200→      type: image.type ?? (image.isVideo ? 'video_travel_output' : 'single_image'),
   201→      metadata: {
   202→        ...image.metadata,
   203→        __autoEnterEditMode: autoEnterEditMode,
   204→        based_on: image.based_on,
   205→      },
   206→    });
   207→    setAutoEnterEditMode(autoEnterEditMode);
   208→  }, [setActiveLightboxMedia, setAutoEnterEditMode]);
   209→
   210→  const handleCloseLightbox = useCallback(() => {
   211→    setActiveLightboxMedia(null);
   212→    setAutoEnterEditMode(false);
   213→  }, [setActiveLightboxMedia, setAutoEnterEditMode]);
   214→
   215→  const handleDownloadImage = useCallback(async (rawUrl: string, filename: string, imageId?: string, isVideo?: boolean, originalContentType?: string) => {
   216→    const currentDownloadId = imageId || filename;
   217→    setDownloadingImageId(currentDownloadId);
   218→
   219→    try {
   220→      await downloadSingleMedia({
   221→        rawUrl,
   222→        filename,
   223→        isVideo,
   224→        originalContentType,
   225→      });
   226→      toast({ title: "Download Started", description: filename });
   227→    } catch (error) {
   228→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
   229→        context: 'useMediaGalleryActions.handleDownloadImage',
   230→        toastTitle: 'Download Failed',
   231→        ...(error instanceof DownloadServiceError ? {
   232→          logData: {
   233→            code: error.code,
   234→            status: error.status,
   235→            statusText: error.statusText,
   236→            url: error.url,
   237→          },
   238→        } : {}),
   239→      });
   240→    } finally {
   241→      setDownloadingImageId(null);
   242→    }
   243→  }, [setDownloadingImageId]);
   244→
   245→  const handleShowTick = useCallback((imageId: string) => {
   246→    setShowTickForImageId(imageId);
   247→    if (mainTickTimeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(mainTickTimeoutRef.current);
   248→    mainTickTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   249→      setShowTickForImageId(null);
   250→    }, INTERACTION_TIMING.galleryTickResetMs);
```

## Comprehensive Trace of `autoEnterInpaint` Chain

I've traced the full `autoEnterInpaint` flow across all entry points. Here's the complete map:

### **1. CALLERS THAT SET `autoEnterInpaint` / `shouldAutoEnterInpaint`**

#### **A. ShotImageManager Lightbox (Shot image gallery)**
- **File:** `ShotImageManagerDesktop.tsx` (line 344-345)

#### **B. MediaGallery Lightbox (Project gallery)**
- **File:** `MediaGalleryLightbox.tsx` (line 355)
  - Passes: `autoEnterInpaint: effectiveAutoEnterEditMode`
  - Computed as: `effectiveAutoEnterEditMode = useMemo(() => fromMetadata ?? autoEnterEditMode ?? false, [...])`
  - Where `fromMetadata = activeLightboxMedia?.metadata?.__autoEnterEditMode` (line 173)

- **Entry Point:** `useMediaGalleryActions.ts` (line 193)
  - `handleOpenLightbox(image, autoEnterEditMode = false)` sets:

      ...image.metadata,
      __autoEnterEditMode: autoEnterEditMode,  // stores in metadata
    }
    ```
  - Called externally when user initiates lightbox open with auto-enter intent

- **External Callers of `handleOpenLightbox`:**
  - Gallery items with "Inpaint" button → passes `autoEnterEditMode: true`
  - MediaGalleryItem double-click → passes default `false`
  - Any external UI that wants to auto-enter can call with `true`

### **2. PROP THREADING CHAIN**

```
┌─ ShotImageManager/MediaGallery/Timeline
├─> MediaLightbox.tsx (accepts autoEnterInpaint? prop)
├─> useBundledLightboxProps() wraps into features bundle
├─> features: LightboxFeatureFlags = { autoEnterInpaint, ... }
├─> Passed to ImageLightbox OR VideoLightbox
└─> ImageLightbox.tsx
    └─> useImageLightboxEditing()
        └─> props.features?.autoEnterInpaint (line 338 in ImageLightbox.tsx)
        └─> Passed to useImageEditOrchestrator()
            └─> mediaContext: { autoEnterInpaint }
            └─> Passed to useInpainting hook
            └─> Which uses useMagicEditMode()
                └─> Passed as autoEnterInpaint param
```

---

### **3. KEY EFFECT IN `useMagicEditMode.ts` (Lines 154-164)**

The actual auto-enter logic:

```typescript
// Auto-enter unified edit mode if requested (only once, not after manual exit)
useEffect(() => {
  // Reset entry guard once we're actually in edit mode
  if (isInpaintMode || isMagicEditMode) {
    isEnteringEditModeRef.current = false;
    return;
  }

  if (autoEnterInpaint && !isVideo && selectedProjectId && !hasManuallyExitedRef.current) {
    handleEnterMagicEditMode();
  }
}, [autoEnterInpaint, isInpaintMode, isMagicEditMode, isVideo, selectedProjectId, handleEnterMagicEditMode]);
```

**Conditions checked:**
1. ✅ `autoEnterInpaint` = true (from props)
2. ✅ `!isVideo` = image (not video)
3. ✅ `selectedProjectId` = project selected
4. ✅ `!hasManuallyExitedRef.current` = user hasn't manually exited edit mode

---

### **4. `usePanelModeRestore.ts` References (Lines 53-73)**

In image lightbox path, `usePanelModeRestore` also checks `autoEnterInpaint`:

```typescript
// Don't restore if initialVideoTrimMode or autoEnterInpaint is set 
// (explicit modes take precedence)
if (initialVideoTrimMode || autoEnterInpaint) {
  hasRestoredPanelModeRef.current = true;
  return;
}
```

**Purpose:** If `autoEnterInpaint` is explicitly set, skip the persisted panel mode restoration (auto-enter takes precedence).

---

### **5. WHERE `shouldAutoEnterInpaint` / `effectiveAutoEnterEditMode` ARE COMPUTED**

| Source | Location | Computation |
|--------|----------|-------------|
| **ShotImageManager** | `useLightbox.ts` line 21 | `useState(false)` – simple boolean |
| **MediaGallery** | `useMediaGalleryActions.ts` line 193 | Passed as param to `handleOpenLightbox(image, autoEnterEditMode)` |
| **MediaGallery Effective** | `MediaGalleryLightbox.tsx` line 172-175 | `useMemo` combines metadata flag + parent prop |
| **Travel Tool** | `useLightbox.ts` line 14 | `useState(false)` |

---

### **FULL REPLACEMENT PLAN: `initialActive` instead of `autoEnterInpaint`**

The refactoring would:

1. **Remove the effect** (useMagicEditMode lines 154-164) that watches `autoEnterInpaint`
2. **Initialize state with `initialActive`** (already supports it, line 99):
   ```typescript
   const [isMagicEditMode, setIsMagicEditMode] = useState(initialActive);
   ```
3. **Pass `initialActive` through the chain:**
   - `autoEnterInpaint` → `initialActive` in mediaContext
   - `useImageEditOrchestrator()` extracts and passes to `useMagicEditMode()`
   - State is initialized immediately on mount

4. **Remove these setters:**
   - `useLightbox.setShouldAutoEnterInpaint(true/false)` calls
   - `setAutoEnterEditMode()` calls in gallery
   - Metadata `__autoEnterEditMode` flag storage/restoration

5. **Keep the manual exit tracking** (`hasManuallyExitedRef`) – this is independent and still valuable to prevent re-opening after user closes

**Benefits:**
- ✅ Deterministic initialization on mount (no effect dependency issues)
- ✅ No effect timing problems or race conditions
- ✅ Cleaner state management (single initialization vs. effect-triggered)
- ✅ No lingering state across media changes
